class QinplaceCoverage(unittest.TestCase):
    """§4.5: every qinplace of a certified region is carried by a covering inverse_pairs entry."""
    TEXT = EX[7] if 7 in EX else None

    def verdict(self, entry_mut=None, claim_mut=None, root_inv=None, entries=None):
        h = hashes.compute_hashes(parse_object(self.TEXT))
        claim = {"kind": "exhaustive", "expected": "inverse_pair",
                 "subject": {"closure_hash": h["g"][1], "variant": "forward", "inherited_controls": 0},
                 "domain": {"args": [rng("a", 2)], "borrowed": [], "bundles": [rng("x", 2), rng("y", 2)], "regions": []}}
        entry = {"g": h["g"][1], "ginv": h["ginv"][1], "claim": 0, "budget": ["3", "0", "0", "0"]}
        ver = {"inverse_pairs": [entry] if entries is None else entries}
        cert, ch, _ = cert_for(self.TEXT, "addya", [claim], ver)
        if entry_mut:
            entry_mut(entry)
        if claim_mut:
            claim_mut(claim)
        if root_inv is not None:
            cert["bindings"]["invocation"] = root_inv
        v = check_certificate(cert, parse_object(self.TEXT), self.TEXT)
        return v, v["regions"][ch]

    def inv(self, tk=("3", "0", "0", "0"), a="1"):
        return {"args": [list(tk), a], "budget": ["3", "0", "0", "0"]}

    def test_root_invocation_covered(self):
        v, r = self.verdict(root_inv=self.inv())
        self.assertEqual(r["errors"], [], r)
        self.assertEqual([s["status"] for s in r["qinplace_coverage"]], ["covered"], r)
        self.assertEqual(v["overall"], "replayed", v)

    def test_larger_tuple_is_covered(self):
        _v, r = self.verdict(root_inv=self.inv(tk=("9", "0", "0", "0")))
        self.assertEqual(r["qinplace_coverage"][0]["status"], "covered", r)

    def test_tuple_below_entry_budget_rejects(self):
        v, r = self.verdict(root_inv=self.inv(tk=("2", "0", "0", "0")))
        self.assertEqual(r["errors"][0]["code"], "E_REPLAY_COVERAGE", r)
        self.assertIn("4.5", r["errors"][0]["section"])
        self.assertEqual(v["overall"], "reject")

    def test_arg_outside_claim_args_domain_rejects(self):
        _v, r = self.verdict(root_inv=self.inv(a="3"),
                             claim_mut=lambda c: c["domain"]["args"][0].update(lo=0, hi=2))
        self.assertEqual(r["errors"][0]["code"], "E_REPLAY_COVERAGE", r)

    def test_swapped_entry_orientation_covers(self):
        h = hashes.compute_hashes(parse_object(self.TEXT))
        _v, r = self.verdict(root_inv=self.inv(),
                             entry_mut=lambda e: e.update(g=h["ginv"][1], ginv=h["g"][1]),
                             claim_mut=lambda c: c["subject"].update(closure_hash=h["ginv"][1]))
        self.assertEqual(r["qinplace_coverage"][0]["status"], "covered", r)

    def test_no_entry_for_the_pair_rejects(self):
        h = hashes.compute_hashes(parse_object(self.TEXT))
        _v, r = self.verdict(entry_mut=lambda e: e.update(ginv=h["g"][1]))
        codes = [e["code"] for e in r["errors"]]
        self.assertIn("E_REPLAY_COVERAGE", codes, r)
        self.assertEqual(r["qinplace_coverage"][0]["status"], "uncovered", r)

    def test_no_inverse_pairs_at_all_rejects(self):
        _v, r = self.verdict(entries=[])
        self.assertIn("E_REPLAY_COVERAGE", [e["code"] for e in r["errors"]], r)

    def test_unbound_tuple_is_unchecked_never_covered(self):
        v, r = self.verdict()
        self.assertEqual(r["errors"], [], r)
        self.assertEqual(r["qinplace_coverage"][0]["status"], "unresolved", r)
        self.assertTrue(r["unchecked"] and r["unchecked"][0]["section"], r)
        self.assertEqual(v["overall"], "unchecked", v)
import copy, json, pathlib
from checker_replay import hashes
from checker_replay.objparse import parse_object
from checker_replay.replay import check_certificate
from tests.spec_vectors import FIXTURES

FIX = FIXTURES / "controlled-action-09"
VERSIONS = {k: "0.9" for k in ("cert", "coverage", "isa", "launch", "object", "sched", "target")}
HEAD = "bennett-object isa=0.9 object=0.9 launch=0.9 sched=0.9 cert=0.9 coverage=0.9 target=0.9 ptr_width=32\n"


def controlled():
    text = (FIX / "controlled.bisa").read_bytes().decode()
    return (text, json.loads((FIX / "certificate.json").read_text()),
            json.loads((FIX / "target.json").read_text()), json.loads((FIX / "schedule.json").read_text()))


def source(fns, data=None, layout=None):
    """Build an object from function text alone, with correct §9.1 signatures and hashes."""
    from checker_replay.objparse import parse_object as P, type_width, region_shape
    text = HEAD + "\nsection symbols\n{}\n\nsection functions\n" + fns
    if data:
        text += "\nsection data\n" + data
    if layout:
        text += "\nsection layout\n" + json.dumps(layout, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    obj = P(text)
    syms = {}

    def mem(group):
        out = []
        for _n, t, role in group:
            A, W = region_shape(t)
            out.append({"A": A, "W": W, "role": role})
        return out
    for i, (name, fn) in enumerate(obj.functions.items()):
        g = fn.groups
        w = lambda grp: [type_width(t, 32) for _n, t, _s in grp]
        if fn.kind == 'oracle':
            sig = {"abi": "0.9", "args": [t for _n, t, _s in g[4]], "fault": w(g[2])[0], "in": w(g[0]), "kind": "oracle",
                   "mem": mem(g[3]), "tgt": w(g[1])[0], "tier": "q"}
        elif fn.kind == 'lproc':
            sig = {"abi": "0.9", "args": [t for _n, t, _s in g[3]], "kind": "lproc", "mem": mem(g[4]), "params": w(g[0]),
                   "result": w(g[1])[0], "tier": "q", "tokens": [t for _n, t, _s in g[2]]}
        else:
            sig = {"abi": "0.9", "args": [t for _n, t, _s in g[3]], "bounds": fn.bounds, "in": w(g[1]), "kind": fn.kind,
                   "mem": mem(g[2]), "mut": w(g[0]), "tier": "q"}
        syms[str(i)] = {"interface_hash": "0" * 64, "kind": fn.kind, "name": name, "signature": sig}
    idx = len(syms)
    for dname, hx in sorted((obj.data or {}).items()):
        syms[str(idx)] = {"interface_hash": "0" * 64, "kind": "data", "name": dname,
                          "signature": {"abi": "0.9", "kind": "data", "size": len(hx) // 2, "tier": "-"}}
        idx += 1
    render = lambda s: text.replace("section symbols\n{}", "section symbols\n" + json.dumps(s, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True))
    h = hashes.compute_hashes(P(render(syms)))
    for s in syms.values():
        s["interface_hash"] = h[s["name"]][0]
    return render(syms)


def cert_for(text, root, claims, verification=None):
    obj = parse_object(text)
    h = hashes.compute_hashes(obj)
    ch = h[root][1]
    rec = {"claims": claims}
    if verification is not None:
        rec["verification"] = verification
    return {"versions": dict(VERSIONS),
            "bindings": {"closure_hash": ch, "dialect_mapping_version": "none", "interface_hash": h[root][0],
                         "object_hash": hashes.object_hash(text), "reference_program_hash": h[root][0],
                         "target_hash": "0" * 64},
            "regions": {ch: rec}}, ch, h


def claim_of(text, cert, ch, i=0, **kw):
    v = check_certificate(cert, parse_object(text), text, **kw)
    return v["regions"][ch]["claims"][i]


def tup(bundles, borrowed=(), regions=()):
    return {"bundles": [str(b) for b in bundles], "borrowed": [str(b) for b in borrowed],
            "regions": [[str(c) for c in r] for r in regions]}


def rng(name, width, lo=0, hi=None):
    return {"name": name, "width": width, "lo": lo, "hi": (1 << width) - 1 if hi is None else hi}

**Versioning.** ISA 0.9, object 0.9, launch 0.9, sched 0.9, cert 0.9,
coverage 0.9, target 0.9. The **full version string** (`0.9`) is part of
every semantic envelope (§9.2): drafts are not compatible with each
other, so every hash of every 0.8 object differs under 0.9 (§0, §16.3).

---

## 0. Changes from 0.8

Every change from the accepted draft 0.8 has one entry below; nothing
changes without an entry. **Kind**: *E* = editorial (wording, or a
reading pinned that a conformant 0.8 implementation could already take,
with local semantics unchanged); *S* = semantic (some outcome or
well-formedness verdict differs from some reading of 0.8, or a
capability is added). **Hash**: every entry rides on the version bump V,
which changes envelope part (1) and every signature's `abi`, hence the
hash of **every** symbol (§9.2): the four 0.8 example hashes all change
and §16.3 republishes their objects (the 0.8 vectors remain valid 0.8
vectors); "+bytes" marks an entry that also changes the canonical bytes
of functions that use the affected form; "new" marks new symbols.
Sources: *EN* = an item of the evidence note
`docs/notes/2026-10-29-isa-09-evidence.md`; *bd* = a bead of this
repository; *SQ n* = entry n of `reference-interpreter/SPEC-QUESTIONS.md`
(its reading is adopted unless the entry says otherwise); *RC* = the
reference-interpreter council round 1
(`docs/council/isa-ref-2026-10-29/round-1/report.md` in `bennett`),
member and finding; *r8* = a council round-8 suggestion (bead
`b-isa-spec-zsw.N`); *DL* = the dialect 0.2 outline's §25 list.

**No narrowing.** No entry rejects, faults, declares inert or removes a
capability that 0.8 permits or the north star promises. The entries
that add or sharpen a well-formedness check (E11's static view rule,
E16.9, E17.2, E17.3, E17.5's unmatched uncalls, E17.8, E17.9, E17.10,
E17.12, E17.14, the block terminators of K5) concern spellings that
always have a legal equivalent or uses that 0.8 did not define soundly
(E17.14: a location value as a classical argument would be superposed);
the run-time checks (E11, K1's width checks, K3's token validity, K4's
inverse-pair check) concern operations that 0.8 did not define or
already required (0.8's `qinplace` already demanded its certificates;
K4 admits library certificates as well as exhaustive claims, as the
north star §3.1 requires).

10. **`any`-class resolution** (§11). Default: the first class in JCS
    order with capacity.
11. **Occupancy rate of a class absent from `rate`** (§8.2). Default: 1.
12. **Classical step timing of CC/R instructions and effects** (§8.2).
    Default: `classical_step_time` each, sequential.
13. **Suite sizes** (§15): the exhaustive widths of suite C and the
    sample counts of `sampled` claims. Default: exhaustive for ≤ 16
    interface bits; 2^16 samples above.
14. **Hardware handling of `qreg.free`** (§3.3). Cleanliness of an
    arbitrary state is not testable nondestructively. Default: on
    hardware the release proceeds without a test and its frame is
    marked unverified, so a segment containing it cannot be uncalled
    (§7.4) and a run containing it is not reverse-eligible (§10);
    releasing a register that was not clean is a program error the
    hardware does not detect *(non-normative: physically its wires are
    then discarded as by an unrecorded measurement; this is a program
    error, not a trace-out operation, §10)*. The same holds for
    `bennett_qreg_prepare` and `bennett_qreg_free` on a non-verifying
    executor. A verifying executor tests and records `FAULT_UB_TRAP` on
    failure (§4.9), or returns `E_QREG_NOT_CLEAN` from the host calls.
15. **Routing and movement cost under a topology** (§8.2–§8.3, §11),
    deferred to 0.10. Default: no routing cost is charged and no routing
    is inserted; a schedule that needs a non-adjacent pair is
    `E_SCHEDULE_INFEASIBLE` naming this item. Absent topology remains
    all-to-all. A producer can supply an explicitly routed gate sequence
    using §4.4 `swap` gates; those gates execute and are charged normally.
    The optional topology schema and adjacency validation are decided in
    0.9; only automatic routing and its movement-cost model are deferred.
16. **Exhaustion of an executor's own classical resources in CC/R**
    (activation stack, history storage; §4.9). CC and R carry no budget
    (§5.4 budgets are Q-tier), so where a finite executor runs out is
    its own limit. Default: a terminal fault record `FAULT_BUDGET_HEAP`
    at the instruction that cannot proceed, raised before any mutation
    by that instruction (§4.9; an executor that cannot guarantee this
    for an exhaustion inside a region call marks the run not
    reverse-eligible); the point is executor-defined and the suites do
    not test it.
17. **Identity-idle step** (§3.6). A variant of the step in which an
    idle step leaves its frame at zero: forward, a = (pc ≠ HALT) into
    clean scratch; dispatch k < n only; rows as printed; the successor
    XORs for k < n and both commit exchanges under a, with no HALT
    successor; then `old_pc ^= HALT` under a (an active frame holds old
    index ⊕ HALT, never 0, since the old index is < n = HALT);

    HALT under a; `old_pc ^= HALT` under a; the two inverse exchanges
    under a; successors and rows undone as printed; dispatch uncomputed
    from pc under a; a cleared from (pc ≠ HALT). Same frame width, same
    F_B and the same postcondition as T2; on pc = HALT with a fresh zero
    frame a forward step is the identity (candidate theorem T7).

**Sampling.** For each domain component with range size R = hi − lo + 1,
assemble m = ⌈⌈log₂ R⌉/64⌉ consecutive generator words (least-significant
word first) into an integer, reduce modulo 2^⌈log₂ R⌉, reject and redraw
if ≥ R, add `lo`; components are consumed in enumeration order.

def _canon_dec(v, path, section="12.3"):
    if not isinstance(v, str) or not DEC.match(v):
        raise _r("E_REPLAY_INVOCATION", section, path, f"{v!r} is not a canonical nonnegative decimal string")
    return int(v)


class _Subject:
    """Interface layout of the claim's subject, independent of its kind."""

    def __init__(self, obj, name, inherited):
        self.obj, self.name, self.inherited = obj, name, inherited
        self.fn = obj.functions.get(name)
        if self.fn is None:
            raise Unchecked(f"subject @{name} has no body in this object", "9.3")
        self.kind = self.fn.kind
        g = self.fn.groups
        pw = obj.ptr_width
        if self.kind == 'oracle':
            self.bundles = list(g[0]) + list(g[1]) + list(g[2])
            self.mem, self.params = g[3], g[4]
            self.roles = ['in'] * len(g[0]) + ['tgt'] + ['fault']
        elif self.kind in ('action', 'perm_action'):
            self.bundles = list(g[0]) + list(g[1])
            self.mem, self.params = g[2], g[3]
            self.roles = ['mut'] * len(g[0]) + ['in'] * len(g[1])
        else:
            raise _r("E_REPLAY_SUBJECT", "12.1", "subject.closure_hash",
                     f"@{name} is a {self.kind}, not a region (§5)")
        self.widths = [type_width(t, pw) for _n, t, _s in self.bundles]
        self.shapes = [region_shape(t) for _n, t, _s in self.mem]
        self.ptypes = [t for _n, t, _s in self.params]
        self.prog = None
        self.borrow_widths = []

    def bind(self, args):
        self.args = args
        if self.kind != 'oracle':
            self.prog = compile_action(self.obj, self.name, args, self.inherited)
            self.borrow_widths = self.prog.borrow_widths
        else:
            self.borrow_widths = self._oracle_borrow_requirement()

    def _oracle_borrow_requirement(self):
        """Oracle bodies have no qborrow row; only a perm_call callee could introduce a borrow."""
        for inst in _closure_rows(self.obj, self.name):
            if inst.op == 'lift.perm_call':
                cname = inst.operands[0][1:]
                if self.obj.functions.get(cname) is None:
                    raise Unchecked(f"lift.perm_call @{cname} is not defined here", "9.3")
                if any(r.op == 'qborrow' for _b, _p, rs in self.obj.functions[cname].blocks for r in _flat(rs)):
                    raise Unchecked(f"@{cname} borrows wires inside an oracle's lifted call; its tuple position "
                                    "is a contract question (K13 borrowed-oracle witness)", "12.1")
        return []

    def run(self, budget, bundles, borrowed, regions):
        """Reference semantics at the bound invocation: the K13 after tuple."""
        if self.kind == 'oracle':
            ctl = bundles[:self.inherited]
            rest = bundles[self.inherited:]
            nin = len(self.fn.groups[0])
            ins, tgt, fault = rest[:nin], rest[nin], rest[nin + 1]
            if all(ctl):
                tgt, fault = oracle_map(self.obj, self.name, ins, tgt, fault, regions, self.args, budget)
            return (ctl + ins + [tgt, fault], list(borrowed), [list(r) for r in regions])
        return self.prog.run(bundles, borrowed, regions)


def _flat(instrs):
    for i in instrs:
        yield i
        for sub in i.nested:
            yield from _flat(sub)


def _closure_rows(obj, name):
    seen, work = set(), [name]
    while work:
        n = work.pop()
        if n in seen or obj.functions.get(n) is None:
            continue
        seen.add(n)
        for _b, _p, rows in obj.functions[n].blocks:
            for r in _flat(rows):
                yield r
                if r.op == 'lift.call':
                    work.append(r.operands[0][1:])


def _validate_budget(obj, budget, path):
    b, heap, stack, depth = budget
    lay = obj.layout
    if lay is None:
        if heap or stack or depth:
            raise _r("E_REPLAY_INVOCATION", "5.4", path, f"memoryless object requires heap=stack=depth=0, got {budget}")
        return

def reject_cited(tc, c, code=None):
    tc.assertEqual(c["outcome"], "reject", c)
    for e in c["errors"]:
        tc.assertTrue(e["section"], e)
    if code:
        tc.assertEqual(c["errors"][0]["code"], code, c)


class PublishedFixture(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.text, self.cert, self.target, self.schedule = controlled()

    def go(self, cert=None, **kw):
        kw.setdefault("target", self.target)
        return claim_of(self.text, cert or self.cert, ROOT, **kw)

    def test_proof_full_domain(self):
        c = self.go()
        self.assertEqual((c["outcome"], c["elements"], c["covered_fraction"]), ("proof", 4, "1"), c)

    def test_non_exhaustive_claims_are_unchecked_with_reason(self):
        v = check_certificate(self.cert, parse_object(self.text), self.text, target=self.target)
        self.assertEqual(v["overall"], "unchecked")
        c = v["regions"][ROOT]["claims"][1]
        self.assertEqual((c["kind"], c["outcome"]), ("numerical", "unchecked"))
        self.assertTrue(c["reason"])
        self.assertTrue(v["unchecked_scope"])
        self.assertEqual(v["summary"], {"proof": 1, "evidence": 0, "reject": 0, "unchecked": 2})

    def test_without_target_realisation_is_unchecked_never_accepted(self):
        c = claim_of(self.text, self.cert, ROOT)
        self.assertEqual(c["outcome"], "unchecked", c)
        self.assertNotIn("covered_fraction", c)

    def test_host_limit_is_unchecked_with_no_fraction(self):
        c = self.go(max_elements=2)
        self.assertEqual((c["outcome"], c["section"]), ("unchecked", "12.2"), c)
        self.assertNotIn("covered_fraction", c)
        self.assertNotIn("elements", c)

    def mutate(self, fn):
        cert = copy.deepcopy(self.cert)
        fn(cert["regions"][ROOT]["claims"][0])
        return cert

    def test_value_mutations_per_claim_field(self):
        t = lambda c: c["expected"]["table"]
        cases = {
            "kind": lambda c: c.update(kind="exhaustive-typo"),
            "unknown_field": lambda c: c.update(extra=1),
            "label_invalid": lambda c: c.update(label="maybe"),

                                               c["expected"].update(table=t(c)[:2])),
            "subject_missing": lambda c: c.pop("subject"),
            "expected_missing": lambda c: c.pop("expected"),
            "subject_closure": lambda c: c["subject"].update(closure_hash="0" * 64),
            "subject_variant": lambda c: c["subject"].update(variant=""),
            "subject_inherited_extra": lambda c: c["subject"].update(inherited_controls=1),
            "subject_schedule": lambda c: c["subject"].update(schedule_hash="0" * 64),
            "domain_name": lambda c: c["domain"]["bundles"][0].update(name="nope"),
            "domain_width": lambda c: c["domain"]["bundles"][0].update(width=2),
            "domain_lo_gt_hi": lambda c: c["domain"]["bundles"][0].update(lo=1, hi=0),
            "domain_hi_out_of_width": lambda c: c["domain"]["bundles"][0].update(hi=2),
            "domain_duplicate": lambda c: c["domain"]["bundles"].append(dict(c["domain"]["bundles"][0])),

            with self.subTest(name=name):

"""Exhaustive-claim replay of a §12 certificate against its object.

Every exhaustive claim is recomputed over its full declared domain from the object
alone. Anything not replayed is reported `unchecked` with a reason, never accepted.
"""
import itertools, re
from fractions import Fraction
from . import hashes as H, jcs, realised
from .action import compile_action
from .errors import ObjectError, Reject, Unchecked
from .objparse import parse_list, region_shape, type_width
from .oracle import oracle_map, oracle_payload

HEX64 = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{64}$')
DEC = re.compile(r'^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$')
F64 = re.compile(r'^0x[0-9a-f]{16}$')
DEFAULT_MAX_ELEMENTS = 1 << 20
MAX_MATERIALISED_A = 20
SCOPE_NOTES = [
    "§12.1 sampled, structural, numerical and channel claims: reported unchecked per claim",
    "§12.3 resources, gates_used, estimate, frame, cleanup, error, composition: not recomputed",
    "§12.3 verification.borrow_frames, unitary, channel, oracle, fault_rate indices: not recomputed",
    "§4.5 qinplace coverage: recomputed only at the root bindings.invocation; other regions' sites are unchecked",
    "§16/suite F object well-formedness: only rules the replay relies on are enforced",
    "§9.3 linked libraries: symbols outside the given object are unchecked",
]


def _r(code, section, path, msg, witness=None):
    return Reject(code, section, path, msg, witness)


def _nat(v, path, section="12.2"):
    """A nonnegative integer as a JSON number or a canonical decimal string (§9.1)."""
    if isinstance(v, bool):
        raise _r("E_REPLAY_SCHEMA", section, path, f"{v!r} is not an integer")
    if isinstance(v, int) and v >= 0:
        return v
    if isinstance(v, str) and DEC.match(v):
        return int(v)
    raise _r("E_REPLAY_SCHEMA", section, path, f"{v!r} is not a nonnegative integer")


def _closed(d, required, optional, path, section):
    if not isinstance(d, dict):
        raise _r("E_REPLAY_SCHEMA", section, path, f"expected an object, got {type(d).__name__}")
    missing = set(required) - set(d)
    if missing:
        raise _r("E_REPLAY_SCHEMA", section, path, f"missing fields {sorted(missing)}")
    unknown = set(d) - set(required) - set(optional)
    if unknown:
        raise _r("E_REPLAY_SCHEMA", section, path, f"unknown fields {sorted(unknown)}")


def _list(v, path, section):
    if not isinstance(v, list):
        raise _r("E_REPLAY_SCHEMA", section, path, f"expected an array, got {type(v).__name__}")
    return v


            if b["target_hash"] != t:
                raise _r("E_REPLAY_BINDING", "12.3", "bindings.target_hash", f"recomputed {t}")
        if "schedule_hash" in b:
            if self.schedule is None:
                raise _r("E_REPLAY_BINDING", "12.3", "bindings.schedule_hash",
                         "bound, but no schedule is embedded or supplied")
            s = H.sha256hex(jcs.canonical(self.schedule))
            if b["schedule_hash"] != s:
                raise _r("E_REPLAY_BINDING", "9.2; 12.3", "bindings.schedule_hash", f"recomputed {s}")
            sb = self.schedule.get("bindings") if isinstance(self.schedule, dict) else None
            root = sb.get("closure_hash") if isinstance(sb, dict) else None
            if root != b["closure_hash"]:
                # §12.1/§12.3: schedule_hash names the ROOT schedule; nested contexts live inside it.
                raise _r("E_REPLAY_BINDING", "11; 12.1; 12.3", "schedule.bindings.closure_hash",
                         f"{root!r} is not the certificate root {b['closure_hash']}"
                         + ("" if root in self.by_closure else " and names no closure of this object"))
            if "invocation" not in b:
                raise _r("E_REPLAY_INVOCATION", "12.3", "bindings.invocation", "a scheduled certificate must carry it")

    def check_region(self, key, rec):
        out = {"errors": [], "claims": [], "unchecked": []}
        if self.by_closure.get(key) is None:
            out["errors"].append(_diag("E_REPLAY_BINDING", "12.3", f"regions.{key}", "key names no symbol's closure hash"))
        if not isinstance(rec, dict) or not isinstance(rec.get("claims"), list):
            out["errors"].append(_diag("E_REPLAY_SCHEMA", "12.3", f"regions.{key}.claims", "claims must be an array"))
            return out
        for i, claim in enumerate(rec["claims"]):
            out["claims"].append(self.check_claim(key, rec, i, claim))
        name = self.by_closure.get(key)
        if name is not None:
            try:
                out["qinplace_coverage"] = self.qinplace_coverage(key, name, rec, out)
            except Reject as e:
                out["errors"].append(e.diag)
            except ObjectError as e:
                out["errors"].append(_diag("E_REPLAY_OBJECT", e.section, "object", e.message))
            except Unchecked as e:
                out["unchecked"].append({"section": e.section, "reason": e.message})
        return out

    # ---- §4.5 qinplace coverage
    def qinplace_coverage(self, key, name, rec, out):
        """Each qinplace in this region's body needs a covering entry of this record (§4.5, §12.3).

        No entry for the pair: reject (the obligation is carried by no entry). With the region's
        invocation bound (bindings.invocation of the root), the tuple of tk and the args are resolved
        and the §4.5 covering test runs; unbound, coverage depends on the caller and is unchecked.
        """
        fn = self.obj.functions[name]
        if fn.kind not in ('action', 'perm_action'):
            return []
        sites = [r for _b, _p, rows in fn.blocks for r in _flat(rows) if r.op == 'qinplace']
        if not sites:
            return []
        vf = rec.get("verification")
        entries = vf.get("inverse_pairs") if isinstance(vf, dict) else None
        entries = [e for e in entries if isinstance(e, dict)] if isinstance(entries, list) else []
        params = [(n, t) for n, t, _s in fn.groups[3]]
        resolved = None
        b = self.cert.get("bindings") if isinstance(self.cert.get("bindings"), dict) else {}
        if b.get("closure_hash") == key and "invocation" in b:
            vals, _budget = _resolve_invocation(self.obj, b["invocation"], [t for _n, t in params], "bindings.invocation")
            resolved = {n: v for (n, _t), v in zip(params, vals)}
        report = []
        for k, ins in enumerate(sites):
            ops = ins.operands
"""Objects for row families the published corpus lacks.

Each case states its relation in `facts` as a hand-derived function, not the replay's
output, so a disagreement cannot be manufactured by the replay agreeing with itself.
Certificates carry the full §12.3 region schema; values outside the replay's scope
are minimal so a checker that rejects them is seen at document level, not claim level.
"""
import itertools
from . import hashes as H, jcs
from .objparse import parse_object, type_width, region_shape

HEAD = "bennett-object isa=0.9 object=0.9 launch=0.9 sched=0.9 cert=0.9 coverage=0.9 target=0.9 ptr_width=8\n"
VERSIONS = {k: "0.9" for k in ("cert", "coverage", "isa", "launch", "object", "sched", "target")}


def build_object(fns, layout=None, data=None):
    text = HEAD + "\nsection symbols\n{}\n\nsection functions\n" + fns
    if data:
        text += "\nsection data\n" + data
    if layout:
        text += "\nsection layout\n" + jcs.canonical(layout).decode() + "\n"
    obj = parse_object(text)

    def mem(group):
        return [dict(zip("AW", region_shape(t)), role=role) for _n, t, role in group]
    syms = {}
    for i, (name, fn) in enumerate(obj.functions.items()):
        g = fn.groups
        w = lambda grp: [type_width(t, obj.ptr_width) for _n, t, _s in grp]
        if fn.kind == 'oracle':
            sig = {"abi": "0.9", "args": [t for _n, t, _s in g[4]], "fault": w(g[2])[0], "in": w(g[0]), "kind": "oracle",
                   "mem": mem(g[3]), "tgt": w(g[1])[0], "tier": "q"}
        elif fn.kind == 'lproc':
            sig = {"abi": "0.9", "args": [t for _n, t, _s in g[3]], "kind": "lproc", "mem": mem(g[4]), "params": w(g[0]),
                   "result": w(g[1])[0], "tier": "q", "tokens": [t for _n, t, _s in g[2]]}
        else:
            sig = {"abi": "0.9", "args": [t for _n, t, _s in g[3]], "bounds": fn.bounds, "in": w(g[1]), "kind": fn.kind,
                   "mem": mem(g[2]), "mut": w(g[0]), "tier": "q"}
        syms[str(i)] = {"interface_hash": "0" * 64, "kind": fn.kind, "name": name, "signature": sig}
    n = len(syms)
    for dname, hx in sorted((obj.data or {}).items()):
        syms[str(n)] = {"interface_hash": "0" * 64, "kind": "data", "name": dname,
                        "signature": {"abi": "0.9", "kind": "data", "size": len(hx) // 2, "tier": "-"}}
        n += 1

    def render(s):
        return text.replace("section symbols\n{}", "section symbols\n" + jcs.canonical(s).decode())
    h = H.compute_hashes(parse_object(render(syms)))
    for s in syms.values():
        s["interface_hash"] = h[s["name"]][0]
    return render(syms)


def _frame(fn):
    g = fn.groups
    slots = []
    if fn.kind == 'oracle':
        for role, grp in (("in", g[0]), ("tgt", g[1]), ("fault", g[2])):
            for i, (_n, t, _s) in enumerate(grp):
                slots.append({"class": "fast", "id": f"{role}:{i}", "role": role, "width": type_width(t, 8)})
    else:
        for role, grp in (("mut", g[0]), ("in", g[1])):
            for i, (_n, t, _s) in enumerate(grp):
                slots.append({"class": "fast", "id": f"{role}:{i}", "role": role, "width": type_width(t, 8)})
    return {"ancillae": [], "borrow": [], "history": [], "scratch": [], "slots": slots}


def certificate(text, root, claim_builder, verification=None):
    obj = parse_object(text)
    hs = H.compute_hashes(obj)
    ch = hs[root][1]
    sym = obj.symbol_by_name[root]
    claims = claim_builder(ch, hs)
    ver = {"borrow_frames": [], "channel": [], "fault_rate": {"domain": {"borrowed": [], "bundles": [], "regions": []},
                                                              "faults_by_code": {}, "layout_dependent_excluded": 0,
                                                              "undefined_excluded": 0},
           "inverse_pairs": [], "oracle": None, "unitary": []}
    if verification:
        ver.update(verification(hs))
    rec = {"bounds": sym["signature"].get("bounds", []), "claims": claims, "cleanup": {}, "definedness_assumptions": [],
           "error": {}, "frame": _frame(obj.functions[root]), "gates_used": {}, "kind": sym["kind"], "resources": {},
           "signature": sym["signature"], "verification": ver}
    return {"bindings": {"closure_hash": ch, "dialect_mapping_version": "none", "interface_hash": hs[root][0],
                         "object_hash": H.object_hash(text), "reference_program_hash": hs[root][0], "target_hash": "0" * 64},
            "composition": {"epsilon_total": {"diamond": 0}, "method": "sum_over_multiplicities", "multiplicities": {}},
            "producer": {"materialisation": {"llvm_version": "none", "normaliser_version": "none", "pipeline": "synthetic"},
                         "name": "checker-replay", "version": "1"},
            "regions": {ch: rec}, "versions": dict(VERSIONS)}


def rng(name, width, lo=0, hi=None):
    return {"name": name, "width": width, "lo": lo, "hi": (1 << width) - 1 if hi is None else hi}


def tup(b, bo=(), r=()):
    return {"bundles": [str(x) for x in b], "borrowed": [str(x) for x in bo], "regions": [[str(c) for c in x] for x in r]}

                label="evidence", regions=None):
